Pretrained global weather models offer an efficient source of large-scale forecasts, motivating their reuse to guide high-resolution regional prediction. However, this coupling requires aligning global and regional representations across different grids and integrating global guidance with local interactions to advance regional states. We propose ScaleCast, a regional forecasting framework that addresses these challenges through Global-Regional Alignment. Its Global-Regional Conversion module aligns joint global and regional representations with regional locations, while the Global-Regional Alignment and Dynamics block combines aligned guidance with regional neighborhood interactions. Experiments using ERA5 global analyses on a 0.25-degree grid and CERRA regional reanalysis at 5.5 km spacing demonstrate improved regional forecasts across surface and upper-air variables, with a single trained model supporting multiple global forecast drivers (i.e., Pangu-Weather, GraphCast, and HRES) without specific retraining. Fine-tuning on HRRR at 3 km spacing further demonstrates the framework's adaptability to a different regional domain and spatial resolution. Windstorm case studies show improved cyclone positioning and core-pressure estimates, while comparisons with HadISD station observations show closer agreement with local temperature and humidity changes.

</details>


### [175] [A Unified Bellman Operator for Safety-Critical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12420)
*Nishanth Arun Rao,Royina Karegoudra Jayanth,Benjamin Eysenbach,Jaime Fernández Fisac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的贝尔曼算子，将性能与安全目标统一到联合价值函数中，并在双时间尺度随机逼近框架下证明其时间差分学习的收敛性，从而在保证安全的前提下最大化任务回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键领域要求强化学习在最大化任务性能的同时严格满足安全约束，但现有安全强化学习范式存在两难：要么依赖先验知识（如安全过滤器）提供严格安全保证，要么支持联合学习但仅在平均意义上满足安全约束。

Method: 提出将性能与安全目标统一为联合价值函数的新型贝尔曼算子；采用双时间尺度随机逼近框架进行时间差分学习：快时间尺度估计学习联合策略的安全价值，慢时间尺度估计联合价值；通过将极限动力学表述为占用平均微分包含来分析收敛性。

Result: 理论上证明该微分包含渐近收敛到一组极限最优安全约束任务价值函数，收敛后的最优策略在始终保持安全的同时最大化任务回报；在连续控制任务上的神经近似实验表明收敛稳定，测试时安全违规接近零。

Conclusion: 该工作通过联合贝尔曼算子和双时间尺度学习统一了性能与安全目标，突破了现有方法在严格安全保证与联合学习之间的权衡，实现了理论上严格安全且稳定的策略学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in safety-critical domains requires maximizing task performance while strictly adhering to safety constraints. Existing safe reinforcement learning paradigms typically force a trade-off: they either require a priori knowledge to provide strict safety guarantees (e.g., safety filters), or they enable joint learning but only satisfy safety constraints on average. In this work, we propose a novel Bellman operator that unifies performance and safety objectives into a joint value function. We show that temporal difference learning with the joint Bellman operator converges under a two-timescale stochastic approximation framework. On the fast timescale, the safety value of the learning joint policy is estimated, while the joint value is estimated on the slow timescale. Convergence is ensured by formulating the limiting dynamics as an occupation-averaged differential inclusion, and showing that it asymptotically converges to a set of limiting optimal safety-constrained task value functions. Theoretically, once converged, the resulting optimal policy maximizes task return while maintaining safety at all times. Empirical evaluations on continuous control tasks with neural approximations demonstrate stable convergence with near-zero safety violations at test time.

</details>


### [176] [Rounding in Preconditioner Space: Redesigning 4-bit AdamW Optimizer-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12444)
*Hanyang Li,Shao Tang,Daniel Thomas Braithwaite,Gregory Dexter,Leonardo Neves,Aman Gupta,Hiroto Udagawa,Abhishek Shivanna,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"舍入空间"视角重新设计AdamW的4-bit优化器状态量化，提出ZIP-SR（零包含+预条件子空间随机舍入）与ZE-EDEN（零排除+重标定）两种方案，在130M–2.7B模型预训练中最大将验证损失差距缩减70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4-bit量化AdamW优化器状态可降低持久存储，但量化误差会通过动量递推传播并扰动后续自适应更新。关键洞察是：量化器选择相邻重建水平的坐标（舍入空间）决定了误差如何在状态空间与预条件子空间中传导，而传统做法默认在状态空间度量误差，可能导致预条件子误差被低估。

Method: 1) 对二阶矩在零点邻近量化单元的局部分析，表明状态均值误差小并不保证下一步预条件子均值误差小；2) 用一维二次型构造展示状态空间舍入与预条件子空间舍入下优化动力学定性不同；3) 提出ZIP-SR：二阶矩码本保留零，并在预条件子空间计算随机舍入概率；4) 提出ZE-EDEN：二阶矩码本排除零，并对量化后的二阶矩块重标定以缓解正量化下限引起的预条件子畸变；5) 两者一阶矩均用4-bit NF4，并在训练最后10%对LM-head一阶矩做定向随机舍入。

Result: 在130M至2.7B参数的GPT与Llama风格预训练实验中，两种方法在各评估规模上均缩小了TorchAO 4-bit AdamW相对32-bit AdamW的平均验证损失差距，最大差距缩减达70%；在全参数监督微调中，两者验证损失低于TorchAO，并在下游任务上接近32-bit AdamW。

Conclusion: 舍入空间是4-bit优化器状态量化的关键设计维度：在预条件子空间度量与执行舍入、并恰当处理二阶矩码本中的零（保留或排除+重标定），能显著抑制量化误差经动量递推的传播，从而在低比特下逼近全精度AdamW的训练与微调表现。

Abstract: Quantizing AdamW's optimizer states reduces persistent storage, but quantization errors propagate through the moment recurrences and perturb subsequent adaptive updates. We redesign 4-bit optimizer-state quantization for AdamW from the perspective of \emph{rounding space}: the coordinate in which a quantizer chooses between adjacent reconstruction levels. For the second moment, a local analysis of the quantization cell adjacent to zero shows that small mean state error need not imply small mean preconditioner error at the next step. A one-dimensional quadratic construction further shows qualitatively different optimization dynamics under state-space and preconditioner-space rounding. These results motivate Zero-Inclusive Preconditioner-space Stochastic Rounding (\textbf{ZIP-SR}), which retains zero in the second-moment codebook and computes stochastic-rounding probabilities in preconditioner space. As a complementary route, Zero-Excluding EDEN calibration (\textbf{ZE-EDEN}) uses a zero-excluding second-moment codebook and rescales the quantized second-moment block to mitigate the preconditioner distortion caused by the positive quantization floor. Both configurations use 4-bit NormalFloat (NF4) for the first moment, with targeted stochastic rounding of the LM-head first moment during the final 10\% of training. Across GPT- and Llama-style pretraining experiments ranging from \textbf{130M} to \textbf{2.7B} parameters, both methods reduce TorchAO 4-bit AdamW's mean validation-loss gap to 32-bit AdamW at every evaluated model size, with the largest reported gap reduction reaching \textbf{70\%}. In full-parameter supervised fine-tuning, both recipes achieve lower validation loss than TorchAO while remaining close to 32-bit AdamW on downstream tasks.

</details>


### [177] [Caught in the Act: Probes Effectively Detect Sabotage and Catch Unverbalized Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12445)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Alex F. Spies,Tigist Diriba,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建迄今最大规模的欺骗数据集FIBS并提出跨层跨token聚合的新型探针架构，将白盒欺骗检测扩展至前沿监控场景，在SHADE-Arena达到98.8% AUC，并能检测"内省型欺骗"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期事件凸显了监控LLM智能体的困难以及模型欺骗人类的风险，因此需要一种可扩展的前沿监控手段。作者希望验证白盒探针式欺骗检测能否在模型规模扩大时仍有效，并突破仅依赖上下文判断欺骗的局限。

Method: 1）收集迄今最大的欺骗数据集FIBS用于训练探针；2）提出新型探针架构，可跨多层与多token聚合信息；3）在SHADE-Arena上与Opus 5.5文本监控基线比较；4）设计"内省型欺骗"评估，即欺骗无法从上下文判断、真值需通过诱导或训练数据知识确定；5）在开放权重模型上测试其对政治敏感话题及受压下信念的谎言检测。

Result: 探针在SHADE-Arena取得98.8% AUC，超过Opus 5.5文本监控基线，且随底座模型规模增大效果提升。在内省型欺骗评估中，探针能以最高99.7% AUC区分含真实隐藏目标的对话记录与其他目标。探针还能检测开放权重模型在政治敏感话题及受压信念上的欺骗。

Conclusion: 白盒探针欺骗检测可有效扩展至前沿监控场景，并具备检测上下文不可判定欺骗的能力。作者公开训练数据集FIBS，以推动探针在前沿部署中的应用，并鼓励社区补充更多欺骗与破坏行为样本。

Abstract: Recent incidents have highlighted the challenge of monitoring LLM agents and the danger of models deceiving people. We show that white-box deception detection via probes can be scaled up to frontier monitoring settings by collecting the largest deception dataset to date for training probes and introducing a novel probe architecture which can aggregate information across many layers and tokens. Our probes achieve 98.8% AUC in SHADE-Arena, surpassing an Opus 5.5 text-monitoring baseline, and show improved efficacy as the underlying model is scaled up. To push our probes to their limit, we test them on several cases where deception cannot be determined from the context alone. In these cases, which we refer to as introspective deception, the ground truth can only be determined through careful elicitation or thorough knowledge of a model's training data. In one such evaluation, we show that probes can distinguish transcripts containing a model's true hidden goal from other goals with an AUC of up to 99.7%. Our probes also readily detect deception on prominent open-weight models which lie about politically sensitive topics, and about their beliefs when put under pressure. We release our training dataset, dubbed FIBS, to help drive frontier deployment of effective probes, and encourage the community to expand upon it with further examples of deception and sabotage.

</details>


### [178] [Bi-FORK: Generative Modeling of High-Dimensional Bifurcating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12449)
*Anna Zimmel,Fleur Hendriks,Markus Holzleitner,Florian Sestak,Martin Weichselbaumer,Vlado Menkovski,Johannes Brandstetter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bi-FORK：一种用于高维物理系统一分多解映射的生成式框架，通过潜空间流匹配生成完整轨迹并结合排斥引导采样，一次性摊销地恢复不同解分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分岔在物理系统中普遍存在（结构屈曲、流体与气候动力学等），但在深度学习中尚未被充分探索。对称破缺分岔处单一输入对应多个同等有效的解，违背了主流学习型物理代理模型所依赖的一对一假设，现有方法无法刻画这种一分多的解映射。

Method: 提出Bi-FORK生成式框架：利用潜空间流匹配（latent flow matching）生成完整轨迹，保持空间与时间连贯性；并采用排斥引导采样（repulsion-guided sampling）在单次摊销推断中恢复不同解分支，从而学习高维系统中的一分多解映射。

Result: 在屈曲梁、机械超材料以及Allen-Cahn相分离三类问题上进行评估，覆盖连续、离散与场值分岔，离散化规模最高达26万点。Bi-FORK成功恢复多模态解结构，且相较此前方法在规模上扩展了数个数量级。

Conclusion: Bi-FORK将生成式建模拓展至高维分岔物理系统，突破了物理代理模型的一对一局限，为学习一分多解映射提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Bifurcations are ubiquitous in physical systems, from structural buckling to fluid and climate dynamics, yet they remain largely unexplored in deep learning. At a symmetry-breaking bifurcation, a single input admits multiple equally valid solutions, violating the one-to-one assumption underlying most learned physical surrogates. We introduce Bi-FORK, a generative framework for learning these one-to-many solution maps in high-dimensional systems. Bi-FORK generates complete trajectories through latent flow matching, preserving space and time coherence, and uses repulsion-guided sampling to recover distinct solution branches in a single amortized pass. We evaluate Bi-FORK on buckling beams, mechanical metamaterials, and Allen-Cahn phase separation, spanning continuous, discrete, and field-valued bifurcations with discretizations up to 260,000 points. Bi-FORK recovers the multimodal solution structure while scaling several orders of magnitude beyond prior approaches, opening generative modeling to high-dimensional bifurcating physical systems.

</details>


<div id='bioRxiv.Neuroscience'></div>

# bioRxiv.Neuroscience [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Synaptic input architecture of visual cortical neurons revealed by large-scale synapse imaging without backpropagating action potentials](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.1101/2024.10.24.619996v2)
*Kondo, S.,Kikuta, K.,Ohki, K.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种去除反向传播动作电位（BAP）信号干扰的新型树突棘钙成像方法，借此大规模记录视觉诱发树突棘活动，揭示出三种树突功能结构模式，并通过建模表明：在"聚集型"树突中，仅需少部分树突棘即可产生锐调谐输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经科学的核心问题之一是：神经元如何整合数千个突触输入并计算出锐调谐的输出。回答该问题需要在体记录突触输入的位置与活动；然而BAP钙信号会侵入树突和树突棘，导致树突棘反应难以被准确记录，这构成关键技术障碍。

Method: (1) 首先开发了一种可在无BAP信号干扰条件下记录树突棘钙反应的新方法；(2) 运用该方法，对小鼠（推断为视觉皮层）L2/3锥体神经元的视觉诱发树突棘活动进行大规模成像；(3) 基于成像结果对突触输入的树突功能结构进行分类，并建立模型评估不同结构下产生锐调谐输出所需的活动棘数量。

Result: 揭示了三种树突功能结构模式：① 具有相似反应聚集（cluster）树突棘的树突；② 树突棘反应多样的树突；③ 多数树突棘无视觉反应的树突。建模结果显示，在聚集型结构的树突中，只需一小部分树突棘即可产生锐调谐输出。

Conclusion: 树突尺度上存在多种突触输入组织模式；聚集型树突结构能以极少的活跃突触输入高效实现锐调谐输出，为神经元整合数千输入产生精确输出的机制提供了树突层面的结构—功能解释。

Abstract: How neurons integrate thousands of synaptic inputs to compute sharply tuned outputs is a critical question in sensory information processing. To answer this question, it is essential to record the location and activity of synaptic inputs in vivo. However, back-propagating action potential (BAP) calcium signals invade dendrites and spines, making accurate recording of spine responses difficult. In this study, we first developed a new method to record spine calcium responses without BAP signals. Using this method, we performed large-scale imaging of visually evoked spine activity from layer 2/3 pyramidal neurons and revealed three patterns of dendritic functional architectures of synaptic inputs: dendrites with clusters of spines of similar responses, dendrites with spines of diverse responses, and dendrites with spines where the majority of them show no visual response. Our model suggests that only a small fraction of spines on dendrites of clustered architectures are sufficient to generate sharply tuned output.

</details>


### [180] [Evidence-Gated Plasticity and Context-Dependent Consolidation for Multi-Goal Learning in Spiking Neural Networks](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.06.25.734613v2)
*Neymotin, S. A.,Hazan, H.,Unal, G.,Earl, C.,Anwar, H.,Franaszczuk, P.,Boothe, D.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文提出证据门控可塑性（EGP）及其目标-情境扩展版本，用于脉冲神经网络中的多目标交错学习，通过多时间尺度可塑性和情境特异性的信用分配显著提升导航学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物启发的脉冲神经网络虽能支持自适应行为，但当不同目标的突触更新相互干扰时，多目标学习变得困难。作者希望探究多时间尺度可塑性与情境特异性信用分配能否改善受内嗅-海马环路启发的脉冲导航系统中的交错多目标学习。

Method: 提出证据门控可塑性（EGP）：由奖励调制的STDP生成瞬时突触提案，再由需求加权的行为证据调节其向持久权重的缓慢巩固。全局EGP跨目标汇聚提案与证据；目标-情境EGP则通过目标特异的提案与证据存储，并对巩固施加额外的需求依赖缩放。研究比较了成对脉冲网络中的算法表现，在关闭可塑性条件下测试学习到的权重，并考察上游表征与巩固各组分。

Result: 全局EGP支持多目标学习与持久导航。目标-情境EGP在40对网络中改善了早期奖励，并在独立指定的30对重复实验中得到验证。在8对训练2000回合的网络中，目标-情境EGP保持奖励优势，提高了目标接触成功率，并在计入失败尝试时缩短了到达目标的期望时间。独立固定权重评估表明两种算法均具持久导航能力，其中8个网络中有7个偏好目标-情境EGP。组分实验复现了目标-情境的奖励优势，并发现情境特异巩固的额外需求依赖缩放是唯一获得调整后统计支持的改进奖励组分。

Conclusion: EGP支持脉冲神经网络中的持久多目标学习，其目标-情境扩展在该交错导航任务中改善了习得效率与条件性目标选择性。该框架为在其他多情境学习问题中检验情境敏感、证据调制的可塑性提供了基础。

Abstract: Background / Introduction: Biologically inspired spiking neural networks offer a mechanistic framework for adaptive behavior, yet learning multiple goals remains challenging when synaptic updates for different targets interfere. We investigated whether multi-timescale plasticity and context-specific credit assignment improve interleaved multi-goal learning in a spiking navigation system inspired by entorhinal-hippocampal circuitry. Methods: We introduce evidence-gated plasticity (EGP), in which reward-modulated spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) generates transient synaptic proposals and need-weighted behavioral evidence modulates their slower consolidation into durable weights. Global EGP pools these proposals and the corresponding evidence across goals. Target-context EGP extends this framework through goal-specific proposal and evidence stores and additional need-dependent scaling of consolidation. We compared the algorithms across paired spiking networks, tested learned weights with plasticity disabled, and examined upstream representations and consolidation components. Results: Global EGP supported multi-goal learning and durable navigation. Target-context EGP improved early reward across 40 paired networks, including an independently specified 30-pair replication. Across eight paired networks trained for 2000 episodes, target-context EGP maintained a reward advantage, increased target-contact success, and reduced expected time to reach the target when unsuccessful blocks were included. Independent fixed-weight evaluation demonstrated durable navigation in both algorithms, with seven of eight networks favoring target-context EGP. A component experiment reproduced the target-context reward advantage and identified additional need-dependent scaling of context-specific consolidation as the only component with adjusted statistical support for improved reward. Conclusions: EGP supports durable multi-goal learning in spiking neural networks, and its target-context extension improves acquisition and conditional target selectivity in this interleaved navigation task. The framework offers a basis for testing context-sensitive, evidence-modulated plasticity in other multi-context learning problems.

</details>


### [181] [Hunger reconfigures a reward learning circuit into a memory competent mode](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.17.752172v2)
*Park, A.,Senapati, B.,Mancini, N.,Zhang, Y.,Choi, J.,Cone, I.,Treiber, C. D.,Costa, R. P.,Fenk, L. M.,Bidaye, S. S.,Waddell, S.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 饥饿通过肽能信号抑制多巴胺基线活动，使奖赏诱发的大振幅多巴胺放电促进学习；持续性大振幅放电将记忆网络切换至优先巩固近期记忆的“饱足”模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内稳态（如饥饿）会动态重塑神经环路活动以支持觅食，而神经调质可调控神经元的生理特性，但这种灵活性如何调控记忆网络尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示饥饿如何重构果蝇多巴胺能食物奖赏环路，并阐明其对记忆形成与巩固的影响。

Method: 以果蝇为模型，对多巴胺能食物奖赏环路进行在体生理记录，比较饥饿与饱足状态下的多巴胺能活动及突触后输出神经元活动模式，并结合行为学学习与记忆测试，分析糖摄入及进食后持续状态对放电模式和记忆网络的影响。

Result: 饥饿经肽能信号抑制多巴胺基线活动，使奖赏诱发的大振幅多巴胺放电得以强化学习；该放电模式可由摄糖触发并持续至进食之后，反映饱足状态，持续的大振幅放电促进学习并将记忆网络切换至优先巩固近期记忆的模式。突触后输出神经元从紧张性、去相关的多巴胺响应模式，转为爆发性、相关的模式，此时进一步的多巴胺调控被遮蔽（occluded）。

Conclusion: 营养剥夺将记忆环路重构为具学习能力、可接收多巴胺信号的模式；随后持续性的强化多巴胺将其切换为驱动记忆巩固的饱足模式。该机制通过生理模式的转换，将状态依赖的奖赏信号与随后的记忆稳定化在机制上相互交织。

Abstract: Internal states such as hunger dynamically reshape activity across circuits to support resource seeking. Neuromodulation provides a means of controlling such physiological properties of neurons, but how this flexibility regulates memory networks remains unclear. Here, we describe how hunger reconfigures a dopaminergic food-reward circuit between two physiological modes that support memory formation, in Drosophila. Starvation suppresses baseline dopaminergic activity through peptidergic signalling, enabling reward-evoked, large-amplitude dopamine neuron spikes to reinforce learning. This spiking mode can be engaged by sugar consumption and persists beyond feeding, reflecting the fly's satiety state. Persistent dopaminergic large-amplitude spiking reinforces learning and transitions the memory network into a mode that prioritizes consolidation of recently acquired memories. The transition from hunger to satiation is also reflected in the activity of postsynaptic output neurons that shift from a tonic, decorrelated mode that is responsive to dopamine into a bursting, correlated mode in which further dopaminergic modulation is occluded. Therefore, nutrient deprivation reconfigures the memory circuit into a learning competent dopamine-receptive mode, which persistent reinforcing dopamine then switches into a satiated mode driving memory consolidation. Together these processes mechanistically intertwine state-dependent reward-signalling with subsequent memory stabilization through transitions in physiological mode.

</details>


### [182] [Inhibitory budget matching constrains homeostatic plasticity in competitive spiking networks](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.27.754763v2)
*Musacchio, F.,Fuhrmann, M.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 在竞争性STDP脉冲网络中，抑制可塑性能否替代固定侧抑制取决于工作点与抑制预算的匹配；预算匹配时慢稳态抑制规则可稳定训练并保持高分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diehl-Cook架构等竞争性脉冲网络依赖固定侧抑制实现竞争，但皮层抑制突触实际具有可塑性并参与兴奋-抑制平衡。尚不清楚在何种条件下生物学上合理的抑制可塑性可以替代固定侧抑制而不破坏表征学习，这一空白构成本文研究动机。

Method: 在MNIST上训练竞争性STDP网络，对比四种抑制机制：固定抑制、Vogels式抑制性STDP（iSTDP）、慢稳态抑制、以及守恒每个兴奋神经元总抑制输入的预算约束慢稳态规则。通过固定抑制工作点图谱筛选参考工作点（g=2.0，wIE=10.0），并在复制初始化上进行参数图谱扫描，比较稳定性、分类精度、放电率与抑制电导统计。

Result: 固定抑制仅56%运行完成训练，高输入增益下更强抑制导致活动失控。参考工作点实现完全稳定且精度84.3±0.6%。该工作点下，预算匹配（BI=3990）的规则在所有复制运行中稳定，精度83.5±0.6%，放电率与抑制电导统计接近固定抑制；预算失配（BI=7980）则每次运行均失控。参数图谱显示iSTDP广泛但非一致稳定（90%完成），慢稳态抑制更稳定（96%）；预算约束稳定性强烈依赖守恒预算，低与匹配预算稳定或近乎稳定，中间预算混合，BI=7980全部失败。

Conclusion: 竞争性STDP网络中的抑制可塑性不能仅靠局部稳态更新决定。稳定可塑竞争同时依赖工作点与固定总竞争强度的抑制预算。机制上，预算匹配将抑制权重的局部分配与总抑制驱动变化分离，防止可塑性将网络推离稳定竞争态。抑制预算匹配被确立为用以可塑、稳态调控的竞争替代固定抑制的实用设计原则。

Abstract: Competitive spiking networks can form sensory representations through local synaptic plasticity and lateral inhibition. A canonical example is the Diehl-Cook architecture, in which Poisson-encoded sensory inputs drive excitatory neurons through spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), while a separate inhibitory population imposes competition between excitatory units. In most implementations this competitive circuit is fixed, although inhibitory synapses are plastic in cortical circuits and contribute to excitation-inhibition balance. It therefore remains unclear under which conditions biologically motivated inhibitory plasticity can replace fixed lateral competition without disrupting representation learning. Here we study this question in a competitive STDP network trained on MNIST. We compare fixed inhibition with a Vogels-style inhibitory STDP rule, slow homeostatic inhibition, and a budget-constrained slow homeostatic rule that conserves the total inhibitory input received by each excitatory neuron. A fixed-inhibition operating-point map showed that stability depends on the dynamical regime: 56% of tested fixed-inhibition runs completed training, whereas stronger inhibition at higher input gain produced runaway activity. The reference regime g = 2.0, wIE = 10.0, selected for subsequent comparisons, combined complete stability across replicate initializations with high classification performance (84.3 {+/-} 0.6% after 30,000 training examples). In this regime, the budget-constrained rule was stable in all replicate runs when its inhibitory budget matched the fixed-inhibition reference (BI = 3990), reaching 83.5 {+/-} 0.6% accuracy with firing-rate and inhibitory-conductance statistics close to fixed inhibition, whereas a mismatched budget (BI = 7980) produced runaway activity in every replicate. Parameter maps showed that Vogels-derived iSTDP was broadly but not uniformly stable (90% runs completed), while slow homeostatic inhibition was even more broadly stable (96%). Budget-constrained stability depended strongly on the conserved budget, i.e., low and matched budgets were stable or nearly stable, intermediate budgets were mixed, and BI = 7980 failed in all tested runs. These results show that inhibitory plasticity in competitive STDP networks is not governed by a local homeostatic update alone. Stable plastic competition depends jointly on the operating regime and on an inhibitory budget that fixes the total strength of competition while allowing local inhibitory weights to adapt. Mechanistically, budget matching separates local redistribution of inhibitory weights from changes in total inhibitory drive, preventing plasticity from shifting the network away from a stable competitive state. This identifies inhibitory budget matching as a practical design principle for replacing fixed inhibition with plastic, homeostatically regulated competition in STDP-based spiking networks.

</details>


### [183] [Upregulation of Calbindin in Adult Inhibitory Neurons Reactivates Critical Period Plasticity in Mouse Visual Cortex](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.07.08.737337v2)
*Nakayama, T.,Figueroa-Velez, D.,England, W.,Miyoshi, E.,Hasselmann, J.,Spitale, R. C.,Swarup, V.,Blurton-Jones, M.,Sack, J. T.,Gardner, T. J.,Gandhi, S. P.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究通过对移植后小鼠初级视觉皮层宿主抑制性神经元进行转录组分析，发现Calb1表达在关键期及移植诱导可塑性中升高，并证明抑制性神经元中Calb1水平决定视觉皮层可塑性程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键期是幼年脑回路高突触可塑性、快速重组的窗口，而成年可塑性需持续的感觉经验改变。GABA能抑制性神经元移植可恢复关键期可塑性，但其分子机制尚不清楚，作者旨在揭示移植诱导可塑性中宿主抑制性神经元的基因表达变化及其功能意义。

Method: 对接受移植的小鼠初级视觉皮层（V1）宿主抑制性神经元进行转录组测序，鉴定差异表达转录本并做GO富集分析；检测候选基因Calb1（钙结合蛋白）在不同移植条件和发育阶段的蛋白表达；构建AAV载体在抑制性神经元中特异性操控Calb1表达，并用内源信号成像测量眼优势可塑性。

Result: 宿主抑制性神经元的差异表达转录本富集于突触可塑性和抑制性神经元发育相关通路；Calb1在V1关键期及移植诱导可塑性期间高表达；抑制性神经元中Calb1水平决定视觉皮层可塑性程度。

Conclusion: 直接诱导抑制性神经元中关键期阶段基因表达模式，可在目标成年皮层回路中恢复幼年可塑性，为成年脑可塑性调控提供分子证据。

Abstract: Critical periods are windows of peak learning performance where heightened synaptic plasticity enables fast, robust reorganization in juvenile brain circuits. Unlike adult plasticity which requires ongoing, persistent change in sensory experience, cortical representations can be rapidly changed during critical periods and have enduring effects. Transplantation of GABAergic inhibitory neurons has been shown to restore critical period plasticity to recipient circuits by triggering signalling changes within host inhibitory neurons. Here we transcriptionally profiled host inhibitory neurons in mouse primary visual cortex (V1) to detect gene expression changes during transplant-induced plasticity. Gene ontology enrichment analysis of differentially-expressed (DE) transcripts in host inhibitory neurons revealed synaptic plasticity- and inhibitory neuron development-related profiles. We assessed the protein expression of a top DE candidate, the calcium-binding protein Calbindin (Calb1), across transplant conditions and developmental stages. We found high Calb1 expression during the V1 critical period and during transplant-induced plasticity. To assess the functional significance of transplant-reactivated DE gene activity on visual cortical plasticity, we developed a set of AAVs to manipulate Calb1 expression specifically within inhibitory neurons. Using intrinsic signal imaging to measure ocular dominance plasticity, we found that Calb1 levels in V1 inhibitory neurons determine the extent of visual cortical plasticity. Our study provides evidence that direct induction of critical period-stage gene expression patterns in inhibitory neurons restores juvenile plasticity in targeted adult cortical circuits.

</details>


### [184] [Environmental dynamics shape human learning: change points versus random walks](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.1101/2025.11.26.690700v2)
*Foucault, C.,Weber, L. A.,Hunt, L. T.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 该研究通过两个行为实验与统一贝叶斯框架比较突变（change point）与渐变（random walk）两种非平稳环境下的学习，发现人类能根据环境的生成结构自适应调整学习率，并在线追踪潜在均值与方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 适应性学习需要结合环境动态来解释预测误差。然而不同环境不仅在"何时变化"上不同，也在"如何变化"上不同：有些非平稳过程渐变漂移，有些则突然跳变。这引出核心问题——人类能否根据环境的生成结构来调整自己的学习策略。

Method: 采用预测推断任务开展两个行为实验，构建统一贝叶斯框架。对比两种典型非平稳环境：突变型变化点与渐变型随机游走。进行交叉验证的模型比较，检验与环境匹配的模型能否更好解释行为；并分析学习率随预测误差大小的动态特征，以及被试对潜在方差（环境随机性）的在线追踪（通过"桨宽"和"位置"的协同调整来体现）。

Result: 交叉验证的模型比较显示，与环境匹配的模型几乎能解释所有被试的行为。人类表观学习率呈现动态特异的特征：在变化点环境下，学习率随预测误差幅度的上升比随机游走环境更陡峭。被试在两种环境中都能在线追踪变化的潜在方差，并与潜在均值联合，通过桨宽与位置的协同调整实现。在变化点环境中，桨宽调整进一步区分了方差变化与均值变化。

Conclusion: 人类能够将学习适应于"潜在状态如何变化"以及"哪些属性正在变化"两方面。研究建立了一个跨不同环境动态的自适应学习统一计算解释。

Abstract: Adaptive learning requires interpreting prediction errors in light of environmental dynamics. However, environments may differ not only in when changes occur, but also in how they occur: some nonstationary processes drift gradually, whereas others change abruptly. This raises the question of whether humans can adapt their learning to the generative structure of their environment. Here, we compared learning in two canonical nonstationary environments: abrupt change points versus gradual random walks. Across two behavioral experiments using a predictive inference task and a unified Bayesian framework, we show that participants adjust their learning to the dynamics of their environment. Cross-validated model comparison showed that the environment-matched model best explained behavior in nearly all participants. Human apparent learning rates also showed the predicted dynamics-specific signature, increasing more sharply with prediction-error magnitude under change points compared to random walks. Participants tracked the changing latent variance (environmental stochasticity) online in both environments, jointly with the latent mean, through coordinated adjustments of paddle width and location. In the change-point environment, their width adjustments further distinguished changes in variance from changes in mean. Together, these results show that humans adapt their learning to both how latent states change and which properties are changing. They establish a unified computational account of adaptive learning across different environmental dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Learning to Orchestrate Evolutionary Search: Progression-Aware Deep Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic DE-CMA-ES Coordination in Optimization and Structural Model Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11546)
*Lechen Li,Rongye Shi,Wanhuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出DRL-DCO算法，用DDPG智能体统一动态调度DE与CMA-ES，实现高维结构模型修正中的自适应探索-开发平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维结构模型修正问题具有复杂非凸、参数相关的特点。现有混合进化算法多依赖静态架构或固定切换规则，导致搜索阶段割裂、协同性差，难以在此类景观中高效搜索。

Method: 提出深度强化学习驱动的动态DE-CMAES编排算法（DRL-DCO）。以DDPG的actor-critic智能体将进化过程作为统一系统持续调控；采用进展感知状态表示和多样性引导奖励；在DE的差分向量探索与CMA-ES的协方差引导开发之间动态分配计算资源，并联合调节种群规模、精英保留与重启机制；训练后的actor可在无监督推理模式下仅通过前向推理自主编排，无需critic评估与权重更新。

Result: 在高维单目标优化基准与IASC-ASCE结构健康监测基准上验证，DRL-DCO在收敛精度与鲁棒性上优于最先进的自适应与混合进化算法，以及单一算子DRL驱动的基线方法。

Conclusion: DRL-DCO通过强化学习统一治理，使算法能在探索主导、开发主导和混合策略之间自主切换，并支持免监督快速部署，为高维结构模型修正提供了有效且鲁棒的求解方案。

Abstract: Solving high-dimensional structural model updating problems requires an algorithm capable of navigating complex, non-convex landscapes with correlated parameters. Existing hybrid evolutionary algorithms typically rely on static architectures or fixed switching rules, resulting in disjointed search phases. To address this, this study proposes a Deep Reinforcement Learning-governed dynamic DE-CMAES Orchestration (DRL-DCO) algorithm, in which a Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG)-based actor-critic agent continuously governs the evolutionary process as a single, unified system rather than a mechanical concatenation of algorithms. Guided by a progression-aware state representation and a diversity-informed reward, the agent fluidly reallocates computational resources between the differencevector-based exploration of Differential Evolution (DE) and the covariance-guided exploitation of CMA-ES, while jointly regulating population size, elite preservation, and a restart mechanism to escape local optima. This allows DRL-DCO to autonomously transition between exploration-dominant, exploitation-dominant, and mixed-strategy regimes across generations. Beyond the training phase, the trained actor can operate in a supervision-free inference mode, where the internalized policy autonomously orchestrates DE and CMA-ES control from observed search states through forward inference alone, without critic evaluation or weight updates, enabling faster deployment while retaining full effectiveness. Validated on high-dimensional single-objective optimization benchmarks and the IASC-ASCE structural health monitoring benchmark, DRL-DCO achieves superior convergence accuracy and robustness compared to state-of-the-art adaptive and hybrid evolutionary algorithms, as well as single-operator DRL-governed baselines.

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Interpretable Memory Models for Spaced Repetition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10548)
*Anders Schill*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出SBD记忆模型，参数比当前最先进模型小80%，准确率几乎持平，且更易解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间隔重复软件的复习安排依赖基于复习日志拟合的记忆模型；仅用测试集准确率不足以衡量模型质量，因为现有数据由旧调度器产生，新方案必须外推超出数据分布，同时模型还需具备简洁的机制性解释。

Method: 提出SBD模型，在保持可解释性与机制性解释的前提下压缩模型规模，参数量比当前最先进方法减少约80%。

Result: SBD在准确率几乎持平于当前最先进模型的同时，参数量缩小80%，并具有更好的可解释性。

Conclusion: 在间隔重复记忆建模中，可通过更小、更可解释的模型达到与最先进方法相当的精度，说明模型质量不应只由测试集准确率评价。

Abstract: Spaced repetition software schedules reviews with a memory model fit to review logs. Accuracy on a test set is not sufficient evidence of quality since available data are produced by existing schedulers, and new solutions must extrapolate beyond them. A model also needs a simple mechanistic interpretation. We present SBD, a model that is more interpretable and 80% smaller than the current state of the art at nearly the same accuracy.

</details>


### [187] [Mean Field Theory Based on the Spike Time Response Curve for Synchronization Within and Between Two Alternating Populations of Neural Oscillators with Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10977)
*Ananth Vedururu Srinivas,Carmen C. Canavier*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文将脉冲耦合振荡器的平均场方法从单个同步群体扩展到两个交替同步群体，用延迟双指数突触的自连接神经振荡器建模，将输入分解为紧张性和时相性分量；由于输入是连续的而非脉冲式，作者放弃振荡器相位概念，仅基于时间间隔扰动推导交替放电模式存在性与稳定性的自洽判据。仿真表明，在高频振荡条件下，该平均场方法比瞬时相位偏移方法更准确地预测两个同步群体间的锁相存在性与稳定性，并可推广至其他非神经振荡器耦合形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究脉冲耦合振荡器时，通常假设每个脉冲的效果是瞬时相位偏移（可能带传导延迟）。但作者关注的是每个动作电位激活双指数突触的神经振荡器，在高频下突触电导持续时间可能超过网络周期，因此接收脉冲的效果会在多个周期内累加，瞬时相位偏移假设失效。作者此前已为单个同步群体定义平均场方法，本文旨在将其扩展到两个交替同步群体。

Method: 将两个交替同步群体问题简化为单个自连接神经振荡器的扰动分析。把每个振荡器发出的延迟双指数突触脉冲序列分为紧张性和时相性两个分量。由于输入是连续的而非脉冲式的，作者放弃振荡器相位概念，仅依据时间间隔相对稳态值的扰动量，推导两同步群体间交替放电模式存在性与稳定性的自洽判据，并通过仿真进行验证。

Result: 仿真结果显示，对于上述高频振荡，平均场方法在预测两个同步群体之间锁相的存在性与稳定性方面，优于瞬时相位偏移方法。

Conclusion: 该平均场方法可有效处理突触电导持续时间超过网络周期的高频脉冲耦合情形，准确刻画两个交替同步群体的锁相行为，并有望推广到非神经振荡器的其他耦合形式。

Abstract: In order to study oscillators coupled by pulses emitted at discrete times, it is generally assumed that the effect of each pulse is an instantaneous phase shift, with a possible conduction delay between emission and receipt. However, we are interested in neural oscillators in which each pulse (action potential) activates a biexponential synapse, and at high frequencies, the duration of the synaptic conductance may be greater than the network period. Therefore, the effect of received pulses can summate over the course of several cycles. To overcome this limitation, we previously defined a mean field approach for a single synchronous population. Here, we extend it to two alternating synchronous populations by assuming a perturbation of a single oscillator from one of the synchronous populations which is represented by a single self-connected neural oscillator. The train of delayed biexponential synapses emitted by each oscillator is divided into a tonic and a phasic component. Because of the continuous rather than pulsatile nature of the input, we had to drop the concept of oscillator phase and derive self-consistent criteria for the existence and stability of an alternating firing pattern between two synchronous populations based solely on the perturbation of time intervals from their steady state values. Simulations showed that for high frequency oscillations as defined above, the mean field approach predicts the existence and stability of phase-locking between two synchronous populations better than the instantaneous phase shift approach. This approach may generalize to other forms of coupling amongst non-neural oscillators.

</details>


### [188] [If the brain were so simple: Information-based drivers of intelligence and their interaction through an n-body-inspired framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11142)
*Brett J. Kagan,Johnson Zhou,Forough Habibollahi,Valentina Baccetti*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文基于信息学与算法热力学，提出"信息焓"及"信息焓势(IEP)"概念，用以刻画神经系统如何以不同方式响应信息模式，并构建了一个可检验、可证伪的框架，将信息焓、自由能原理与神经临界性统一起来，用以解释智能行为的产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于大脑如何处理信息并产生智能输出的理论众多，但往往难以得到决定性的检验。作者希望建立一种以信息学和算法热力学为基础的研究路径，聚焦信号信息熵与系统内部结构化信息资源之间的关系，以期为现有理论（如自由能原理）提供可整合且可证伪的补充框架。

Method: 提出新概念"信息焓"，表示系统内部可用于预测和其他形式做功的结构化信息资源；引入"信息焓势(IEP)"作为度量外部信号提升系统信息焓能力的指标；提出并实现了一种在给定信号中量化IEP的方法，并在多种示例信号上进行评估；同时给出神经系统中信息焓处理的生物学猜想，并将其与自由能原理及神经临界性相结合。

Result: 成功提出并实现了对信号中信息焓势(IEP)的量化方法，并在多类示例信号上进行了验证；论证了该框架可与自由能原理整合并为其提供可证伪性，同时阐明了其与神经临界性的关系；提出了一个受n体模型启发的、可检验可证伪的框架。

Conclusion: 该框架描述了各驱动因素中的不同机制通过n体启发式模型相互作用，支配神经系统在高维状态空间中的"运动"；作者据此提出，这些特征构成了神经驱动因素最基础的部分，进而产生了被称为"智能"的复杂自适应行为，并为相关理论提供了可检验、可证伪的研究纲领。

Abstract: Theories of how the brain processes information and returns intelligent outputs are numerous and often difficult to conclusively test. Here, we consider an approach grounded in informatics and algorithmic thermodynamics for how neural systems respond to different patterns of information in different ways, with a focus on the relationship between the information entropy of a signal and a new proposed quantity we term information enthalpy, which represents the internal resource of structured information within a system available to be used for predictions and other forms of work. To evaluate the capacity of the external signals to increase information enthalpy in the system, we introduce the information enthalpy potential (IEP) as a defined metric. We propose and implement a method for quantifying the amount of potential information enthalpy - the IEP - in a given signal across a range of example signals. We offer a conjecture of how information enthalpy may be treated by neural systems within a biological framework before showing how it may integrate and offer falsifiability to existing approaches such as the Free Energy Principle. This framework is also positioned in light of the role of neural criticality. Finally, we postulate a testable and falsifiable framework where the distinct mechanisms within each driver interact through a described n-body-inspired model to govern the "motion" of a neural system through a high dimensional state-space. Through these processes, it is proposed that these features form the most fundamental basis of the neural drivers that give rise to the complexity of adaptive behaviors that are commonly called: intelligence.

</details>


### [189] [Neural Decoding as Cognitive Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11923)
*Yi Guo,Changhong Jing,Yong Hu,Yan Liu,Michael K. P. Ng,Shanshan Wang,Shuqiang Wang*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该文将神经解码重构为受脑内在先验约束的认知推断，生成元神经语义表征，从而从多变神经活动中恢复稳定认知状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑能在神经活动持续变化时维持稳定认知，但如何从可变神经观测中提取稳定认知状态仍是神经解码的核心难题。现有方法基于刺激—反应原则，将神经观测映射到预设外部标签，易捕获特定记录中的伪相关。

Method: 受贝叶斯大脑理论启发，将神经解码重述为由脑内在先验约束的认知推断，获得高层元神经语义表征；在五种神经记录模态、三个认知领域（运动、感知与内部心理活动）中开展解码实验。

Result: 该方法重塑了神经观测表征的几何结构，所得元神经语义表征在不同认知任务间呈现一致的几何关系，并能从可变神经观测中恢复稳定认知状态。

Conclusion: 认知稳定性通过对变化神经活动的认知推断得以维持，无需固定神经活动模式，为大脑在外部环境持续变化下保持相对稳定认知提供了机制性解释。

Abstract: The brain maintains stable cognition despite continuously changing neural activity. How to extract stable cognitive states from variable neural observations remains a central problem in neural decoding. Existing neural decoding methods map neural observations to predefined external labels based on the stimulus-response principle, often capturing recording-specific spurious correlations. Inspired by how the brain infers the world, and specifically by Bayesian brain theory, we recast neural decoding as cognitive inference constrained by brain-intrinsic priors, yielding high-level meta-neural semantic representations. In decoding experiments spanning five neural recording modalities and three cognitive domains (motor, perception and internal mentation), our cognitive inference method reorganized the geometry of neural observation representations, yielding meta-neural semantic representations that exhibited consistent geometric relationships across cognitive tasks and enabled the recovery of stable cognitive states from variable neural observations. Our work provides an account of how the brain maintains relatively stable cognition despite continual changes in the external environment. Cognitive stability is sustained through cognitive inference from changing neural activity, without requiring fixed neural activity patterns.

</details>

### [190] [Polarization of the Drosophila oocyte by active cortical exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10916)
*Mohammadhossein Firouznia,Wen Lu,Margot Lakonishok,Reza Farhadifar,Vladimir I. Gelfand,Michael J. Shelley*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究结合定量显微、光转换实验、扰动实验与生物物理建模，揭示果蝇卵母细胞中oskar mRNA的后极定位由位置依赖的皮层交换与胞质扩散共同决定，其模式受oskar总量与皮层解离/结合速率比（交换平衡常数）两个参数控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果蝇卵母细胞前后极性依赖oskar mRNA的后极定位。oskar-RNP在胞质运输、皮层结合/解离以及微管密度前后梯度之间存在复杂的“皮层拔河”，但胞质运输与皮层结合/解离如何耦合产生稳定的后极帽尚不清楚。

Method: 整合定量显微成像、光转换（photoconversion）实验、实验扰动（如改变解离速率）以及生物物理建模；模型纳入RNP的去混合（demixing）倾向，并以总oskar量和皮层解离/结合速率比（交换平衡常数）为关键参数。

Result: 在野生型oskar体积分数下，测得的交换常数使oskar定位于后极皮层而不发生去混合；若平衡被打破（如提高解离速率），oskar会不可逆地去混合成胞质液滴，这与实验扰动观察一致。

Conclusion: 在oskar RNP自吸引的背景下，体相扩散与皮层交换可将细胞骨架不对称性转化为稳健的发育极性。

Abstract: Anterior-posterior polarization in the Drosophila oocyte requires posterior localization of oskar mRNA. oskar mRNA is produced in the anteriorly situated nurse cells, moved into the oocyte, and assembled into oskar-Staufen ribonucleoprotein particles (RNPs). These RNPs then move through the oocyte and interact with the cortex, where myosin-V binds them to the actin network and kinesin-1 removes them along cortically anchored microtubules. This cortical tug-of-war takes place against an anterior-to-posterior decrease in microtubule density. How the interplay between cytoplasmic transport and cortical binding and unbinding produces a stable posterior cap is unclear. Here, we combine quantitative microscopy, photoconversion experiments, experimental perturbations, and biophysical modeling to show that posterior localization emerges from position-dependent cortical exchange coupled to cytoplasmic diffusion. Our model, which accounts for the tendency of RNPs to demix, suggests that two key parameters that determine the observed localization pattern are the total amount of oskar and the ratio of cortical unbinding to binding rates (the exchange equilibrium constant). Given oskar volume fractions in wild-type, the measured exchange constant is such that oskar localizes to the posterior cortex without demixing. If the balance is broken, say by increasing unbinding rates, oskar irreversibly demixes into cytoplasmic droplets. This is consistent with observations from our experimental perturbations. Our results show how bulk diffusion and cortical exchange, in the face of oskar RNPs' self-attraction, convert a cytoskeletal asymmetry into robust developmental polarity.

</details>


### [191] [Three-dimensional imaging of isolated membrane-protein complexes in vacuo with an X-ray laser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11729)
*Kartik Ayyer,Tej Varma Yenupuri,Johan Bielecki,Lutz A. Eichacker,J. Domingo Meza-Aguilar,Louise J. Persson,Lena Worbs,Patrick Adams,Roberto Alvarez,Adil Ansari,Alfredo Bellisario,Rebecca Boll,Carl Caleman,Sebastian Cardoch,Andrea Castoldi,Joe P. J. Chen,Cyril Danilevski,Merle Dechering,Simon Dold,Armando D. Estillore,Alberto De Fanis,Lukas V. Haas,Patrick Konold,David Lomitze,Abhishek Mall,Dimitra Manatou,Iñaki de Diego Martinez,Parichita Mazumder,Andrew J. Morgan,Anna Munke,Yevheniy Ovcharenko,Matteo Porro,Safi Rafie-Zinedine,Amit K. Samanta,Christina Schmidt,Arezu Sehati,Jonas A. Sellberg,Björn Senfftleben,Carolin Seuring,Zhou Shen,Nicusor Timneanu,Sergey Usenko,Daniel Westphal,August Wollter,Tamme Wollweber,Paul Lourdu Xavier,Henry N. Chapman,Janos Hajdu,Richard A. Kirian,Jochen Küpper,Erik G. Marklund,Petra Fromme,Michael Meyer,Filipe R. N. C. Maia*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 利用欧洲XFEL与氦气交换降低背景散射，从32788张单颗粒衍射图重建出1-MDa光系统I三聚体的3D电子密度，分辨率达3.8 nm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XFEL有望实现单生物分子、病毒和细胞的超快成像，但小颗粒的弱散射信号易被残余气体背景淹没，此前仅能应用于病毒、细胞器等高散射目标，亟需降低背景以拓展至小尺寸单颗粒。

Method: 采用电荷还原电喷雾电离将光系统I三聚体气溶胶化并注入欧洲XFEL光束，通过部分氦气交换使背景散射降低80%；从32788张随机取向单三聚体衍射图重建三维电子密度。

Result: 重建得到分辨率3.8 nm（受探测器几何限制）的三维电子密度，呈约22 nm宽、10 nm厚的盘状，尺寸与去垢剂胶束中PS I三聚体一致，并与真空中分子动力学模拟预测的压缩及原生质谱观测相符。

Conclusion: 结果表明膜蛋白复合物可在真空中用X射线激光成像，是迈向单大分子超快衍射成像的重要一步。

Abstract: The prospect of imaging single biomolecules, viruses and cells with intense, ultrashort X-ray pulses has driven the development of X-ray free-electron lasers (XFELs). However, the weak scattering from small particles is easily swamped by background from residual gas, which has so far limited applications to strongly scattering targets such as viruses, cell organelles and cells. Here we report a three-dimensional (3D) reconstruction of an isolated 1-MDa membrane-protein complex, photosystem I (PS I), from single-particle diffraction data. PS I trimers were aerosolised by charge-reduction electrospray ionisation and injected into the European XFEL beam, with partial helium gas exchange reducing background scattering by 80%. From 32 788 diffraction patterns of single trimers in random orientations, we reconstructed the 3D electron density to a resolution of 3.8 nm, limited by the detector geometry. The disc-shaped density, about 22 nm across and 10 nm thick, matches the size of a PS I trimer in a detergent micelle and is consistent with the compaction predicted by molecular dynamics simulations of the complex in vacuo and observed in native mass spectrometry. These results show that membrane-protein complexes can be imaged in vacuo with X-ray lasers, an important step towards ultrafast diffractive imaging of single macromolecules.

</details>


### [192] [Femtosecond Three-Dimensional Imaging of Single-Protein with Hard X-ray Laser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11892)
*Paul Lourdu Xavier,Andrew J. Morgan,Johan Bielecki,Amit K. Samanta,Simon Dold,Chufeng Li,Xinyue Gao,Spencer K. Passmore,Ruslan P. Kurta,Tim B. Berberich,Emiliano De Santis,Wenhui Zhang,Juncheng E,Faisal H. M. Koua,Romain Letrun,Luca Gelisio,Oleksii Turkot,Egor Sobolev,Lukas V. Haas,Jannik Lübke,Jingxuan He,Madeline B. F. Memovich,Kevin Janson,Stefanie Lenzen,Safi Rafie-Zinedine,Mansi Butola,Oleksandr Yefanov,Fabian Trost,Chan Kim,Tomas Popelar,Tokushi Sato,Yevheniy Ovcharenko,Rebecca Boll,Sergey Usenko,Thomas M. Baumann,Alberto De Fanis,Björn Senftleben,Matteo Porro,Andrea Castoldi,Ibrahym Dourki,Cyril Danilevski,Ekaterina Round,Christina Schmidt,Huijong Han,Roberto Alvarez,Erin C. Yang,Shunzhi Wang,Marcos C. Miranda,Hao Shen,Alexis Courbet,Nathan M. Ennist,Ervin Chia,Cornelia Cazey,Carolin Seuring,Ruojie Sha,Maia Azubel,David A. Bushnell,Sébastien Boutet,Sergey Ovchinnikov,Adrian P. Mancuso,Michael Meyer,Richard Bean,Anton Barty,Filipe R. N. C. Maia,Richard A. Kirian,Thomas D. Grant,N. Duane Loh,Carl Caleman,Erik G. Marklund,Nadia Zatsepin,Andrew V. Martin,Saša Bajt,Roger D. Kornberg,David Baker,Jochen Küpper,Henry N. Chapman*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用X射线自由电子激光（XFEL），首次对单个分子复合物——巨型血红蛋白erythrocruorin（Ery）实现了单颗粒成像，二维衍射图可达15 Å分辨率，三维图像约20 Å，突破了辐射损伤限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XFEL超强脉冲可实现辐射敏感样品的成像，并有望实现类似冷冻电镜但无需冷冻固定、具飞秒至毫秒时间分辨率的生物分子单颗粒成像。然而此前的生物单颗粒成像流程仅在大病毒颗粒上得到验证，尚未在单个分子复合物上实现。

Method: 基于X射线束聚焦、颗粒输运、衍射探测及先进分析方法的数十年改进，采用XFEL脉冲对单个分子复合物巨型血红蛋白erythrocruorin（Ery）进行单颗粒衍射成像，并将二维衍射图分类及重构为二维与三维图像。

Result: 二维衍射图类别可重构至15 Å分辨率，三维图像约20 Å，而倒空间三维合并强度延伸超过20 Å。两者分辨率差异可能源于气相压缩导致复合物的异质性。

Conclusion: 若提高通量，该方法可用于质谱研究中揭示生物分子的原位结构细节；改进样品输运后，有望实现天然态生物单颗粒的超快快照成像，超越辐射损伤限制。

Abstract: The extremely intense pulses of X-ray free-electron lasers (XFELs) have enabled imaging of radiation-sensitive samples, such as macromolecular microcrystals, beyond radiation damage limits. These sources have the potential to deliver biomolecular single-particle imaging, similar to cryo-electron microscopy but without the need for cryo-fixation and with temporal resolution from femtoseconds to milliseconds. While this possibility was recognized before XFELs were built, the biological single-particle imaging work-flow has previously only been demonstrated on large virus particles. Based on decades of improvements in X-ray beam focusing, particle delivery, diffraction detection, and advanced analysis, here we demonstrate imaging of a single molecular complex, the giant-hemoglobin erythrocruorin (Ery) with X-ray laser pulses. Two-dimensional classes of diffraction patterns could be reconstructed to 15 Angstrom resolution, and 3D images to approximately 20 Angstrom, while the 3D merged intensity in reciprocal space extended beyond 20 Angstrom. The resolution discrepancy is likely due to heterogeneity caused by gas-phase compaction of the complexes. With increased throughput, this approach could be used to reveal in-situ structural details during mass spectrometry studies of biomolecules, while improvements in sample delivery may provide ultrafast snapshot imaging of biological single-particles in their native-state beyond the limitations of radiation damage.

</details>
